Interface Contract (real) \u2014 `POST /functions/v1/youtube-data`\n\n**Auth:** Bearer JWT do usu\u00e1rio (validado via `auth.getUser()`); conta YouTube resolvida owner-scoped server-side. Uma \u00fanica edge fn, dispatch por `body.action`.\n\n| `action` | Request (campos al\u00e9m de `action`) | Response 200 | Gate de escopo |\n|----------|-----------------------------------|--------------|----------------|\n| `channel_summary` | \u2014 | `{ channel }` (item completo ou null) | `youtube.readonly` |\n| `list_videos` | `uploadsPlaylistId?`, `pageToken?` | `{ videos[], nextPageToken, uploadsPlaylistId }` | `youtube.readonly` |\n| `video_metrics` | `startDate?`, `endDate?`, `metrics?`, `dimensions?`, `videoId?`, `sort?`, `maxResults?` | `{ columnHeaders[], rows[] }` | `yt-analytics.readonly` **OU** `-monetary` (superset) |\n| `video_categories` | `regionCode?` (default `BR`) | `{ categories[] }` | `youtube.readonly` |\n| `revenue_metrics` | `startDate?`, `endDate?`, `metrics?` | `{ columnHeaders[], rows[], monetized }` (`monetized:false` + `reason` no fail-soft) | `yt-analytics-monetary.readonly` |\n| `update_video` | `videoId` (obrigat\u00f3rio), `title?`, `description?`, `tags?` | `{ updated:true, video }` | `youtube.force-ssl` |\n| `delete_video` | `videoId` (obrigat\u00f3rio) | `{ deleted:true, videoId }` | `youtube.force-ssl` |\n\n**Error codes (reais, todos verificados no c\u00f3digo e cobertos pelo smoke):**\n\n| Code | Condition | Body |\n|------|-----------|------|\n| 401 | JWT ausente/inv\u00e1lido; ou token irrecuper\u00e1vel p\u00f3s-refresh | `{ error:\"Unauthorized\" }` / `{ error:\"youtube_token_unavailable\", action }` |\n| 400 | `action` ausente \u00b7 `videoId` ausente em write \u00b7 action desconhecida | `{ error:\"action required\" \\| \"videoId required\" \\| \"unknown_action\" }` |\n| 403 | Escopo faltando (fail-closed, ANTES do Google) \u00b7 quota Google excedida | `{ error:\"youtube_scope_missing\", missing[], action }` / `{ error:\"youtube_quota_exceeded\", \u2026 }` |\n| 404 | `update_video` de v\u00eddeo inexistente | `{ error:\"video_not_found\" }` |\n| 409 | Nenhuma conta YouTube ativa | `{ error:\"youtube_not_connected\", action:\"Conecte seu canal\u2026/dashboard/social\" }` |\n| 502 | Erro n\u00e3o-quota da API Google | `{ error:\"youtube_api_error\", status, reason, message }` |\n| 500 | Lookup da conta falhou \u00b7 erro interno | `{ error:\"account_lookup_failed\" \\| \"youtube_data_failed\" }` |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Data Requirements\n\n**Zero tabela nova.** O data model do m\u00f3dulo \u00e9 **100% reuso** (decis\u00e3o DEC-YT-002) \u2014 detalhado e reconciliado em [`06-data-model.md`](06-data-model.md), incluindo as **duas fontes de m\u00e9tricas YouTube** que coexistem no ecossistema:\n\n1. **Painel (este m\u00f3dulo):** leitura **on-demand** da Analytics API v2 via `youtube-data` \u2014 nunca persiste m\u00e9trica.\n2. **Coletor (`publication_metrics`, migration `20260626130000`):** snapshot **por publica\u00e7\u00e3o** (`platform='youtube'`, FR-VA-027, never-fabricate) \u2014 s\u00e9rie temporal de outcome de posts publicados pelo MCORCH.\n\nSuperf\u00edcies reusadas: `social_accounts` (+ view `decrypted_social_accounts`; `scopes[]` = sinal de verdade do gating) \u00b7 `social_app_config` (app creds BYOK do OAuth) \u00b7 `infra_health_logs` (`service='youtube-studio'`, `event='video_deleted'`) \u00b7 `mcorch_nodes` (first-sync + auditoria de delete) \u00b7 cross-m\u00f3dulo: `channel_profiles` youtube short/long_video (`20260627140000`) \u00b7 `channel_variants` \u00b7 `src/lib/format-specs.ts:110,116,122` (`youtube_short`/`youtube_video`/`youtube_community` \u2014 SSOT client de formato/preview, Amendment 21).\n\n---\n\n## 6. Architecture Overview\n\n**Princ\u00edpio arquitetural (o que de fato foi constru\u00eddo):** o painel \u00e9 um **proxy fino, stateless e owner-scoped** sobre as APIs do Google \u2014 um **chokepoint \u00fanico** (`youtube-data`) que resolve o token OAuth per-user server-side, aplica o gate de escopo **fail-closed antes** de tocar o Google, e nunca persiste dado de canal (leitura on-demand; DEC-YT-002). vive num \u00fanico funil audit\u00e1vel; adicionar uma action \u00e9 adicionar um branch *depois* dos gates, nunca um caminho novo que possa esquec\u00ea-los. env usado s\u00f3 para infra (`SUPABASE_URL`, `SB_SECRET_KEY`, `SB_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`) |\n| Sinks | `infra_health_logs` (pulse `:100-101`; `event='video_deleted'` `:342-344`) \u00b7 `mcorch_nodes` (`:204-222`, `:345-351`) \u2014 ambos best-effort, nunca bloqueiam a resposta |\n\n### 2.2 Hooks e p\u00e1ginas (Harness Engineering \u2014 l\u00f3gica no hook, p\u00e1gina display-only)\n\n| Component | File | Responsabilidade | FR |\n|-----------|------|------------------|----|\n| `useYouTubeConnection` + queries/mutations | `src/hooks/useYouTubeStudio.ts` (204 linhas) | Deriva o estado de conex\u00e3o/escopos da view mascarada (`:104-127`); `hasMonetaryScope` \u21d2 `hasAnalyticsScope` (superset `:111-112`); queries gated; `useUpdateVideo`/`useDeleteVideo` (`:189-203`) | FR-YT-023/025 |\n| `YouTubeStudioPage` | `src/pages/YouTubeStudioPage.tsx` (589 linhas) | Display-only: cards, grade, dialogs; `canWrite` gating `:300`; AlertDialog `:404-425`; CTA `:503-512`; Receita `:579-584` | FR-YT-025/021/026 |\n| Rota | `src/App.tsx:30,131` | Lazy `/dashboard/youtube` dentro de `DashboardLayout` | \u2014 |\n\n### 2.3 Adjac\u00eancias (rails de outros m\u00f3dulos que este m\u00f3dulo pressup\u00f5e)\n\n| Rail | File | Papel aqui |\n|------|------|-----------|\n| OAuth social | `supabase/functions/social-auth-init/index.ts:125-157` | Pedido dos **4 escopos** + `access_type=offline&prompt=consent` (`:157` \u2014 sem `prompt=consent`, re-auth devolve refresh_token NULL em sil\u00eancio); app creds per-user via `resolveSocialAppCreds` fail-closed |\n| OAuth callback | `supabase/functions/social-auth-callback/index.ts` | Persiste `scopes[]` concedidos + tokens cifrados (Vault) \u2014 o array \u00e9 o **sinal de verdade** de todo o gating |\n| Token refresh | `supabase/functions/refresh-social-token` | Rota\u00e7\u00e3o auditada; invocado por `ensureFreshToken` com service key |\n| Publish | `supabase/functions/publish-social/index.ts:502+` | `videos.insert` resumable (post-engine); disclosure hardcoded `:538-539` \u2014 a classifica\u00e7\u00e3o por-upload \u00e9 FR-YTS-006 (Track B) |\n| Channel Format Intelligence | migration `20260627140000:132,:169` + `scripts/qa/smoke-longform-16x9.ts` | 2\u00ba trilho YouTube vivo: variantes nativas short/long_video de pilar (baseline 16:9 do Track B) |\n| M\u00e9tricas por publica\u00e7\u00e3o | migration `20260626130000` (`publication_metrics`) | 2\u00aa fonte de m\u00e9tricas (coletor snapshot) \u2014 reconciliada com a leitura on-demand do painel em [`06-data-model.md`](06-data-model.md) |\n| Formatos client | `src/lib/format-specs.ts:110,116,122` | SSOT de `youtube_short`/`youtube_video`/`youtube_community` (chromes de preview, Amendment 21) |\n\n### 2.4 QA / Regress\u00e3o\n\n| Item | Path | Prova |\n|------|------|-------|\n| Smoke fail-closed | `scripts/qa/smoke-youtube-write-ops.ts` | 9 gates S1\u2013S9, zero-custo, **zero chamadas ao Google** (usa throwaway users; prova o contrato do funil: 401/400/409/403\u00d73/400\u00d72/400) |\n| Bateria consolidada | `scripts/qa/guard-sweep.sh:33` | `youtube-write-ops (force-ssl gate, 9)` integrado \u2014 regress\u00e3o roda com o sweep de produ\u00e7\u00e3o |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Data Flow (fluxos reais)\n\n### 3.1 Ciclo de leitura (FR-YT-001..004, 022)\n\n```\nUser \u2192 YouTubeStudioPage \u2192 useYouTubeStudio (queries gated por escopo derivado)\n  \u2192 POST youtube-data { action }   [Bearer JWT]\n    \u2192 auth.getUser() \u2192 401 se inv\u00e1lido\n    \u2192 decrypted_social_accounts .eq(user_id) .eq(platform,'youtube') .eq(is_active,true)\n        \u2192 409 youtube_not_connected se ausente\n    \u2192 scope gate (:137-164) \u2192 403 youtube_scope_missing { missing[] } se gap   \u2190 ANTES do Google\n    \u2192 ensureFreshToken (refresh <60s de expirar, via refresh-social-token)\n    \u2192 Google Data v3 / Analytics v2 (token per-user)\n        \u2192 erro \u2192 googleError: 403 quota \u2192 youtube_quota_exceeded \u00b7 resto \u2192 502 youtube_api_error\n    \u2192 pulse('healthy') + [1\u00ba channel_summary: observation node first-sync, dedup por nome \u2014 a729f83]\n    \u2192 200 payload\n```\n\n### 3.2 Ciclo de escrita \u2014 update (FR-YT-020)\n\n```\nEditar (dialog) \u2192 useUpdateVideo \u2192 youtube-data { action:'update_video', videoId, title?, description?, tags? }\n  \u2192 gates idem 3.1 (force-ssl exigido)\n  \u2192 GET videos?part=snippet&id=  \u2192 404 video_not_found se ausente\n  \u2192 merge: title/description do body OU snippet atual \u00b7 tags do body OU atuais \u00b7\n    categoryId SEMPRE preservado do snippet atual (a API substitui o part inteiro e exige a categoria)\n  \u2192 PUT videos?part=snippet \u2192 200 { updated:true } \u2192 invalidate ['youtube','videos']\n  Owner-scope: o token \u00e9 do pr\u00f3prio usu\u00e1rio \u2014 o Google 403-a v\u00eddeo alheio e googleError o superficializa limpo.\n```\n\n### 3.3 Ciclo destrutivo \u2014 delete (FR-YT-021 + FR-YT-027)\n\n```\nExcluir (bot\u00e3o) \u2192 AlertDialog \"Excluir v\u00eddeo permanentemente?\" (:404-425 \u2014 Cancelar / Excluir permanentemente)\n  \u2192 useDeleteVideo \u2192 youtube-data { action:'delete_video', videoId }\n  \u2192 gates idem 3.1 (force-ssl) \u2192 DELETE videos?id=  (204-tolerante :338)\n  \u2192 telemetria distingu\u00edvel (a729f83): infra_health_logs { service:'youtube-studio', status:'healthy',\n    event:'video_deleted' }  \u2190 SEM payload per-tenant (tabela global-read)\n  \u2192 trilha de auditoria: mcorch_nodes { user_id, node_type:'observation',\n    name:'youtube-studio:delete:<videoId>', metadata:{ kind:'youtube_delete', video_id } }  \u2190 RLS-scoped\n  \u2192 200 { deleted:true, videoId }\n  N\u00e3o h\u00e1 undo na API \u2014 a confirma\u00e7\u00e3o da UI \u00e9 o gate real. Dry-run + type-to-confirm = FR-YT-026 (deferida, Fatia A3).\n```\n\n### 3.4 Reconex\u00e3o OAuth (FR-YT-024)\n\n```\nPainel detecta gap (409/403) \u2192 card \u00e2mbar \u2192 \"Reconectar com permiss\u00f5es ampliadas\" (:503-512)\n  \u2192 social-auth-init platform=youtube \u2192 authUrl com os 4 escopos + offline + prompt=consent (:151-157)\n  \u2192 Google consent (lista exatamente os 4) \u2192 social-auth-callback persiste scopes[] + tokens (Vault)\n  \u2192 useYouTubeConnection re-deriva os booleans \u2192 superf\u00edcies aparecem\nEstado vivo: reconex\u00e3o do Usu\u00e1rio Zero FEITA 2026-07-15T18:00:05Z com os 4 escopos (prova datada wf_2998d4c7).\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Security Design\n\n### 4.1 Threat Model (STRIDE \u2014 controles reais)\n\n| Component | Threat | Type | Mitigation shipada (evid\u00eancia) |\n|-----------|--------|------|-------------------------------|\n| `youtube-data` | Chamada sem/with JWT forjado | Spoofing | `auth.getUser()` no gateway da fun\u00e7\u00e3o (`:104-111`) \u2192 401 |\n| Lookup de conta | Leitura cross-tenant da view decriptada (service-role bypassa RLS) | Info Disclosure | Filtro `.eq('user_id', user.id)` **load-bearing** (`:119-125`) \u2014 a fronteira de tenant \u00e9 o filtro expl\u00edcito, documentado como tal no c\u00f3digo |\n| Token OAuth | Vazamento ao client | Info Disclosure | Token resolvido e usado s\u00f3 server-side; o client consome a view **mascarada**; refresh_token nunca manuseado no chokepoint (rota\u00e7\u00e3o delegada a `refresh-social-token`) |\n| A\u00e7\u00f5es al\u00e9m do consentimento | Chamada com escopo n\u00e3o concedido | Elevation | Scope gate fail-closed ANTES do Google (`:137-164`); espelho na UI (controles invis\u00edveis sem escopo \u2014 `:300`, `:579`) \u2014 a UI nunca promete o que o token n\u00e3o permite |\n| `update_video` | Corrup\u00e7\u00e3o de metadados (PUT substitui o part) | Tampering | Read-modify-write preservando `categoryId` (`:313-326`) |\n| `delete_video` | Destrui\u00e7\u00e3o irrevers\u00edvel acidental / indistingu\u00edvel | Tampering / Repudiation | `videoId` expl\u00edcito obrigat\u00f3rio (400 sem ele) + AlertDialog; `event='video_deleted'` + n\u00f3 de auditoria com videoId (a729f83) \u2014 um delete nunca mais parece um list no HUD |\n| `delete_video` em massa | Fan-out destrutivo | Tampering | Inexistente por constru\u00e7\u00e3o: 1 action = 1 videoId; nenhum caminho batch |\n| V\u00eddeo alheio | Write em recurso de outro canal | Elevation | Owner-scope pelo pr\u00f3prio token do usu\u00e1rio \u2014 o Google rejeita (403) e `googleError` superficializa estruturado |\n| `infra_health_logs` | Vazamento per-tenant em tabela global-read | Info Disclosure | O event `video_deleted` **n\u00e3o carrega payload** \u2014 o dado sens\u00edvel (videoId, user) vai para a mesh RLS-scoped (decis\u00e3o consciente do reparo) |\n| Quota Google | Esgotamento do teto di\u00e1rio compartilhado | DoS (self) | Path de leitura quota-eficiente (~4 un/refresh \u2014 uploads playlist, nunca `search.list`); erro 403-quota vira `youtube_quota_exceeded` com orienta\u00e7\u00e3o pt-BR |\n\n### 4.2 Postura fail-closed consolidada\n\n1. **Sem JWT \u2192 401.** 2. **Sem conta \u2192 409 com CTA.** 3. **Sem escopo \u2192 403 estruturado com `missing[]` e CTA \u2014 antes de qualquer byte ao Google.** 4. **Sem token utiliz\u00e1vel p\u00f3s-refresh \u2192 401 com CTA.** 5. **Erro Google \u2192 estruturado (403-quota/502), nunca engolido.** 6. **Telemetria/mesh s\u00e3o best-effort** \u2014 falha nos sinks jamais degrada a opera\u00e7\u00e3o do usu\u00e1rio (`.then(() => {}, () => {})`).\n\nO smoke S1\u2013S9 \u00e9 o contrato execut\u00e1vel desta postura (verificado: cada code path acima tem um gate correspondente).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Design Decisions (com racional \u2014 todas seladas pelo shipado)\n\n| # | Decis\u00e3o | Racional | Alternativa rejeitada |\n|---|---------|----------|----------------------|\n| D1 | **Chokepoint \u00fanico com dispatch por `action`** | Gates de seguran\u00e7a num s\u00f3 funil; smoke prova o contrato inteiro; adicionar action nunca cria caminho sem gate | 1 edge fn por m\u00e9todo (N funis para auditar; o 11 \u00a76 chegou a sugerir \"edge functions `youtube-*`\" no plural \u2014 n\u00e3o foi o constru\u00eddo) |\n| D2 | **Painel stateless, leitura on-demand (zero cache)** | Dado de canal muda no Google, n\u00e3o no MCORCH; cache criaria staleness + superf\u00edcie RLS nova sem benef\u00edcio no volume atual (~4 un/refresh torna o on-demand barato) | Cache de metadados por `project_id` (citado no 11 \u00a76 #8 \u2014 **nunca existiu**; DEC-YT-002) |\n| D3 | **Billing 0 mco** (DEC-YT-001) | Gerir o pr\u00f3prio canal \u2260 gerar conte\u00fado; custo marginal real = quota (USD 0); cobrar leitura do pr\u00f3prio dado seria anti-produto | D\u00e9bito por a\u00e7\u00e3o (afirmado no 11 \u00a77 \u2014 **nunca codado**; qualquer d\u00e9bito futuro exige `COIN_COSTS` + Amendment) |\n| D4 | **4 escopos exatos, sem `youtube.upload`** | `videos.insert` \u00e9 coberto pelo escopo `youtube` (amplo); `yt-analytics-monetary` \u00e9 **superset** do n\u00e3o-monet\u00e1rio \u21d2 1 escopo de analytics cobre `video_metrics` E `revenue_metrics`; cada escopo mapeia a opera\u00e7\u00e3o demonstr\u00e1vel no v\u00eddeo de verifica\u00e7\u00e3o Google (Amendment 12 \u00a71) | Manter upload + analytics n\u00e3o-monet\u00e1rio (5 escopos \u2014 conjunto do SOP antigo, STALE) |\n| D5 | **Scope gate server-side + espelho client** | O servidor \u00e9 a autoridade (fail-closed); o client deriva os mesmos booleans da view mascarada s\u00f3 para **n\u00e3o renderizar** promessas vazias \u2014 defesa em profundidade sem confiar no browser | Gate s\u00f3 no client (bypass\u00e1vel) ou s\u00f3 no server (UX de 403 cru) |\n| D6 | **Delete = AlertDialog simples** (Amendment 12 \u00a73) | Proporcional ao Usu\u00e1rio Zero single-tenant; destrava o gate Y4 da verifica\u00e7\u00e3o Google sem construir UX especulativa | Dry-run + type-to-confirm (11 \u00a77 Fatia 3) \u2014 **deferido honesto** como FR-YT-026/Fatia A3, n\u00e3o abandonado |\n| D7 | **`monetized:false` fail-soft no revenue** | O 403 do Google em canal n\u00e3o-monetizado N\u00c3O \u00e9 falha do app \u2014 a chamada em si j\u00e1 demonstra o escopo; crash puniria o usu\u00e1rio por n\u00e3o ser parceiro YPP | Propagar o 403 (quebraria o card e o v\u00eddeo de demonstra\u00e7\u00e3o) |\n| D8 | **Uploads playlist (1 un) em vez de `search.forMine` (100 un)** | 100\u00d7 mais barato + `search` tem teto pr\u00f3prio de 100/dia; grade de at\u00e9 50 v\u00eddeos hidrata em 2 chamadas (~4 un o refresh completo) | `search.list` (estouraria o teto com ~100 refreshes) |\n| D9 | **Sinks best-effort (telemetria + mesh)** | Observabilidade nunca pode custar disponibilidade; o dado per-tenant vai para a mesh (RLS), o pulso agregado para o HUD (global-read) | Escrita s\u00edncrona bloqueante (acoplaria o painel \u00e0 sa\u00fade do Supabase write path) |\n| D10 | **Guarda-chuva 2 tracks no mesmo slug** (Decis\u00e3o Sovereign 2026-07-16) | O painel (shipado) e a f\u00e1brica (vis\u00e3o) compartilham canal, OAuth e quota \u2014 separar slugs duplicaria BoK e OTDs; separar **ids** (`FR-YT` \u00d7 `FR-YTS`) impede que roadmap contamine estado | Slug novo para a f\u00e1brica (fragmentaria a rastreabilidade da verifica\u00e7\u00e3o Google, que \u00e9 uma s\u00f3) |\n\n---\n\n## 6. Infrastructure Touchpoints\n\n| Infra | Uso | Evid\u00eancia |\n|-------|-----|-----------|\n| `infra_health_logs` | `service='youtube-studio'` healthy/degraded em cada path; `event='video_deleted'` distingu\u00edvel no delete | `youtube-data:100-101`, `:342-344` |\n| `mcorch_nodes` (Knowledge Mesh) | First-sync observation node (dedup `youtube-studio:first-sync:<uid>`) + audit node por delete (`youtube-studio:delete:<videoId>`) \u2014 Mesh Connection Mandate CLAUDE.md \u00a73 **fechado 2026-07-16** | `:204-222`, `:345-351`; commit `a729f83` |\n| Supabase Vault | Tokens OAuth cifrados; leitura s\u00f3 pela view service-role `decrypted_social_accounts` | rail social (migrations do social OAuth) |\n| `guard-sweep.sh` | Regress\u00e3o do funil na bateria consolidada de produ\u00e7\u00e3o | `:33` |\n| Quota Google | Or\u00e7amento: ~4 un/refresh \u21d2 ~2.500 refreshes/dia no teto 10.000 (11 \u00a75.1 permanece v\u00e1lido) | `youtube-data:233-262` |\n| **N\u00c3O usados** (por design) | pg_cron (sem job) \u00b7 `deduct_mco_coins` (0 mco) \u00b7 tabelas novas (stateless) \u00b7 `verify_jwt=false` (a fun\u00e7\u00e3o usa o gateway JWT padr\u00e3o + `auth.getUser()`) | \u2014 |\n\n---\n\n## 7. Estado vivo datado (2026-07-16) \u2014 gates do Amendment 12\n\n| Gate | Estado | Prova |\n|------|--------|-------|\n| Y1 (init com 4 escopos; consent lista os 4) | **FECHADO** | `social-auth-init:151-157` deployado; reconex\u00e3o concedeu os 4 |\n| Y2 (`youtube-data` deployado; writes gated force-ssl; revenue gated monet\u00e1rio; superset) | **FECHADO** | C\u00f3digo vivo `:137-164`; smoke S4\u2013S6 |\n| Y3 (reconex\u00e3o concede os 4 \u2192 painel mostra Receita + Editar/Excluir) | **FECHADO 2026-07-15/16** \u2014 reconex\u00e3o FEITA (updated_at 2026-07-15T18:00:05Z) + YouTube Analytics API HABILITADA + `reports.query` HTTP 200 `rows=[[61]]` | Prova datada auditoria `wf_2998d4c7` (refuta como STALE as pend\u00eancias de HANDOFF:178/252) |\n| Y4 (edi\u00e7\u00e3o real muda t\u00edtulo; exclus\u00e3o de teste some do canal) | **ABERTO \u2014 witness manual Sovereign** | Usar upload descart\u00e1vel; prova ocular |\n| Y5 (`/security-review`) | **FECHADO** na fatia A2 | Amendment 12 \u00a75 |\n| Y6 (tsc 0 \u00b7 build deployado) | **FECHADO** | Deploy do reparo `a729f83` (2026-07-16) |\n| Google-side (fora do app) | **ABERTOS \u2014 gated Sovereign**: branding no console (janela 7d) \u00b7 data-access verification (v\u00eddeo demo) \u00b7 classifica\u00e7\u00e3o sensitive/restricted + CASA `(n\u00e3o verificado)` | FRD \u00a77.1 |\n\n---\n\n## 8. Track B \u2014 postura de design (roadmap, N\u00c3O shipado)\n\nO design da f\u00e1brica long-form **n\u00e3o \u00e9 selado aqui** \u2014 a semente \u00e9 o blueprint 00 e o gate Closed-Loop exigir\u00e1 SDD pr\u00f3pria (ou Amendment desta) quando o Track B sair do roadmap. Registram-se apenas as \u00e2ncoras que o design futuro herda:\n\n- **Runtime:** reuso do padr\u00e3o worker-host (`video-bridge`/`voice-bridge`), **n\u00e3o** um 3\u00ba substrato; o unlock central \u00e9 o `engine='faceless'` hoje 503-gated **corretamente** (`video-render:104-113` \u2014 nenhum deduct antes de engine dispon\u00edvel; FM-YTS-08 j\u00e1 mitigado pelo rail existente).\n- **Baseline vivo:** render 16:9 determin\u00edstico single-render J\u00c1 existe (`render-core.ts:90` template `viral-long-16x9`; E2E `smoke-longform-16x9.ts` G1\u2013G4) \u2014 o Track B come\u00e7a no **assembler multi-cena**, n\u00e3o no render.\n- **Decis\u00e3o aberta que BLOQUEIA o modo avatar:** **OTD-YTS-002** (Hedra \u00d7 MuseTalk) \u2014 tens\u00e3o real com o m\u00f3dulo `avatar-clone-ai`; decis\u00e3o Sovereign pendente; **nenhum c\u00f3digo de lipsync antes dela**.\n- **Custo:** `faceless:125` foi calibrado para Short; long-form exige nova chave 4\u00d7-floor medida (OTD-YTS-008) antes de qualquer cobran\u00e7a.\n- **Disclosure:** evoluir as constantes vivas de `publish-social:538-539` para input por-upload computado (FR-YTS-006) \u00e9 requisito de monetiza\u00e7\u00e3o (FM-YTS-01/02), n\u00e3o cosm\u00e9tico.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Pattern Conformance Declaration (Closed-Loop Step 3.5 \u2014 OBRIGAT\u00d3RIA)\n\n> SSOT: `docs/architecture/agentic-vision.md` \u00a72 (21 padr\u00f5es). **Declara\u00e7\u00e3o, n\u00e3o sign-off.** Esta tabela **promove a do 11 \u00a76 corrigindo-a contra o c\u00f3digo vivo** (auditoria `wf_2998d4c7`): **#8** vira `yes` **leg\u00edtimo s\u00f3 p\u00f3s-reparo `a729f83`** (antes era declara\u00e7\u00e3o indevida); **#12/#16/#18** perdem claims nunca codadas (retry/backoff, d\u00e9bito mcoCoins, confirma\u00e7\u00e3o dupla); **#13** documenta o shipado real (AlertDialog). Escopo: Track A (painel). O Track B declarar\u00e1 conformidade pr\u00f3pria quando ganhar c\u00f3digo.\n\n| # | Pattern | Implemented? | How / Why-deferred |\n|---|---------|:---:|---|\n| 1 | Prompt Chaining | **n-a** | Painel \u00e9 proxy CRUD data-driven sobre a API do Google; zero prompt LLM no m\u00f3dulo (gera\u00e7\u00e3o vive upstream em `orchestrate-*`) |\n| 2 | Routing | **yes** | Dispatch determin\u00edstico das 7 actions no chokepoint + resolu\u00e7\u00e3o de credencial per-user antes de qualquer env (API Tenancy Model): conta \u2192 token \u2192 escopo \u2192 Google (`youtube-data:113-176`) |\n| 3 | Parallelization | **yes** | Queries TanStack independentes (canal \u00b7 v\u00eddeos \u00b7 m\u00e9tricas \u00b7 receita) rodam concorrentes no client; hidrata\u00e7\u00e3o em lote de 50 IDs numa chamada (`:253-259`) \u2014 fan-out bounded pelo pr\u00f3prio shape da API |\n| 4 | Reflection | **deferred** | Sem Generator-Critic aqui (OTD-AG-003); Vision-QA aplica-se a criativos gerados, n\u00e3o \u00e0 grade de dados |\n| 5 | Tool Use | **yes** | Data API v3 + Analytics v2 como ferramentas tipadas encapsuladas por action \u00fanica com contrato de erro estruturado (FRD \u00a74); superf\u00edcie catalogada no 11 \u00a72\u2013\u00a74 |\n| 6 | Planning | **yes** | Fatiamento A1\u2192A2 com gates de materialidade Y1\u2013Y6 (Amendment 12 \u00a75) executado na ordem TIER (leitura antes de escrita antes de destrutivo); esta su\u00edte BoK sela o plano retroativamente |\n| 7 | Multi-Agent | **deferred** | Painel single-surface; delega\u00e7\u00e3o Pantheon (ex.: modera\u00e7\u00e3o de coment\u00e1rios) sem FR \u2014 diferida honestamente (a superf\u00edcie de comments est\u00e1 catalogada, n\u00e3o prometida) |\n| 8 | Memory Management | **yes** *(leg\u00edtimo desde 2026-07-16, commit `a729f83`)* | Observation node `youtube-studio:first-sync:<uid>` no 1\u00ba `channel_summary` (dedup determin\u00edstico, best-effort \u2014 `:204-222`) + n\u00f3 de auditoria RLS-scoped por delete (`:345-351`). **Corre\u00e7\u00e3o do 11 \u00a76:** o \"cache de metadados por project_id\" declarado l\u00e1 **nunca existiu** (painel stateless por design \u2014 DEC-YT-002);
